ERROR: [Place 30-675] Sub-optimal placement for a global clock-capable IO pin and BUFG pair.If this sub optimal condition is acceptable for this design, you may use the CLOCK_DEDICATED_ROUTE constraint in the .xdc file to demote this message to a WARNING. However, the use of this override is highly discouraged. These examples can be used directly in the .xdc file to override this clock rule.
	< set_property CLOCK_DEDICATED_ROUTE FALSE [get_nets u_clkgen/g_hw.u_ibufds/O] >

	Clock Rule: rule_gclkio_bufg
	Status: FAILED
	Rule Description: An IOB driving a BUFG must use a GCIO in the same clock region as the BUFG

	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_ibufds/IBUFCTRL_INST (IBUFCTRL.O) is locked to IOB_X0Y82
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_bufg_in (BUFGCE.I) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on BUFGCE_X0Y42

	The above error could possibly be related to other connected instances. Following is a list of 
	all the related clock rules and their respective instances.

	Clock Rule: rule_bufg_mmcm_1load
	Status: PASS 
	Rule Description: A BUFGCE with I/O driver driving a single MMCM must both be in the same clock region
	if CLOCK_DEDICATED_ROUTE=BACKBONE is NOT set
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_bufg_in (BUFGCE.O) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on BUFGCE_X0Y42
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_mmcm (MMCME4_ADV.CLKIN1) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on MMCM_X0Y1

	Clock Rule: rule_bufgce_bufg_conflict
	Status: PASS 
	Rule Description: Only one of the 2 available sites (BUFGCE or BUFGCE_DIV/BUFGCTRL) in a pair can be
	used at the same time
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_bufg_in (BUFGCE.O) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on BUFGCE_X0Y42

	Clock Rule: rule_bufg_driven_by_fabric
	Status: PASS 
	Rule Description: A BUFGCE clock buffer whose input is driven by a non IO/Clock element should not
	be placed in BUFGCE_HDIO sites.
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_ibufds/IBUFCTRL_INST (IBUFCTRL.O) is locked to IOB_X0Y82
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_bufg_in (BUFGCE.I) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on BUFGCE_X0Y42

	Clock Rule: rule_mmcm_bufg
	Status: PASS 
	Rule Description: A MMCM driving a BUFG must be placed in the same clock region of the device as the
	BUFG
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_mmcm (MMCME4_ADV.CLKOUT0) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on MMCM_X0Y1
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_bufg_out (BUFGCE.I) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on BUFGCE_X0Y24

	Clock Rule: rule_bufgce_bufg_conflict
	Status: PASS 
	Rule Description: Only one of the 2 available sites (BUFGCE or BUFGCE_DIV/BUFGCTRL) in a pair can be
	used at the same time
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_bufg_out (BUFGCE.O) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on BUFGCE_X0Y24

Resolution: A dedicated routing path between the two can be used if: (a) The global clock-capable IO (GCIO) is placed on a GCIO capable site (b) The BUFG is placed in the same bank of the device as the GCIO pin. Both the above conditions must be met at the same time, else it may lead to longer and less predictable clock insertion delays.

However, the use of this override is highly discouraged. These examples can be used directly in the .xdc file to override this clock rule.

	Rule Description: An IOB driving a BUFG must use a GCIO in the same clock region as the BUFG

	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_ibufds/IBUFCTRL_INST (IBUFCTRL.O) is locked to IOB_X0Y82
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_bufg_in (BUFGCE.I) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on BUFGCE_X0Y76

	The above error could possibly be related to other connected instances. Following is a list of 
	all the related clock rules and their respective instances.

	Clock Rule: rule_bufg_mmcm_1load
	Status: PASS 
	Rule Description: A BUFGCE with I/O driver driving a single MMCM must both be in the same clock region
	if CLOCK_DEDICATED_ROUTE=BACKBONE is NOT set
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_bufg_in (BUFGCE.O) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on BUFGCE_X0Y76
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_mmcm (MMCME4_ADV.CLKIN1) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on MMCM_X0Y3


	Clock Rule: rule_bufgce_bufg_conflict
	Status: PASS 
	Rule Description: Only one of the 2 available sites (BUFGCE or BUFGCE_DIV/BUFGCTRL) in a pair can be
	used at the same time
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_bufg_in (BUFGCE.O) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on BUFGCE_X0Y76


	Clock Rule: rule_bufg_driven_by_fabric
	Status: PASS 
	Rule Description: A BUFGCE clock buffer whose input is driven by a non IO/Clock element should not
	be placed in BUFGCE_HDIO sites.
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_ibufds/IBUFCTRL_INST (IBUFCTRL.O) is locked to IOB_X0Y82
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_bufg_in (BUFGCE.I) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on BUFGCE_X0Y76


	Clock Rule: rule_mmcm_bufg
	Status: PASS 
	Rule Description: A MMCM driving a BUFG must be placed in the same clock region of the device as the
	BUFG
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_mmcm (MMCME4_ADV.CLKOUT0) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on MMCM_X0Y3
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_bufg_out (BUFGCE.I) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on BUFGCE_X0Y92


	Clock Rule: rule_bufgce_bufg_conflict
	Status: PASS 
	Rule Description: Only one of the 2 available sites (BUFGCE or BUFGCE_DIV/BUFGCTRL) in a pair can be
	used at the same time
	u_clkgen/g_hw.u_bufg_out (BUFGCE.O) is provisionally placed by clockplacer on BUFGCE_X0Y92

Resolution: A dedicated routing path between the two can be used if: (a) The global clock-capable IO (GCIO) is placed on a GCIO capable site (b) The BUFG is placed in the same bank of the device as the GCIO pin. Both the above conditions must be met at the same time, else it may lead to longer and less predictable clock insertion delays.
